# Package file listings — `ghc`

Auto-generated **recursive** listing of files contained in each failing RPM URL mapped to this component. Archives inside the RPM (tarballs, zips, etc.) are recursively expanded — a leaf entry of the form `outer.tar.gz/inner.zip/path/to/file.txt` means `file.txt` lives inside `inner.zip`, which lives inside `outer.tar.gz`, which lives directly in the RPM payload.

Notes in `[brackets]` after a path indicate special entries: symlinks, opaque archive formats we cannot extract (e.g. `.7z`), or unreadable archives.
